  rounds[0].matches[0].eventBetTypes[2].eventBetTypeItems[1].canBeSimulated = false

################################################################################
#                                                                              #
#               COMPREHENSIVE API EXPLORATION SUMMARY                         #
#                                                                              #
################################################################################

## WORKING ENDPOINTS (status 200 with data):

1. GET /8035/matches
   - Returns: { rounds: [...], betTypes: [...] }
   - Contains 10 rounds (current betting round = 10, future rounds 11-19 with 0 matches)
   - Round structure: { expectedStart, expectedEnd, roundNumber, matches[], id, eventCategoryId }
   - Match structure: { id, entryPointId, round, name, awayTeam, homeTeam, expectedStart, eventBetTypes[] }
   - NO score/result fields in match objects (these are future/unplayed matches)
   - eventBetTypes contains: 1X2, HT/FT, Score Exact, Double Chance, Over/Under, G/NG, etc.
   - canBeSimulated is ALWAYS false on all bet items
   - Response size: ~335KB

2. GET /8035/ranking  
   - Returns: { teams: [...] }
   - Team structure: { name, points, position, history[], won, lost, draw }
   - history is array of last 5 results: ["Won", "Lost", "Draw", ...]
   - Response size: ~2.4KB

3. GET /8035/results?skip=0&take=200
   - Returns: { rounds: [...], hasMore: boolean }
   - Contains 9 completed rounds (1-9) with full score data
   - Match structure DIFFERS from /matches:
     { id, entryPointId, name, awayTeam, homeTeam, goals[], score, halfTimeScore, expectedStart }
   - goals[] array: [{ minute, homeScore, awayScore, team: "Home"|"Away" }]
   - score: string like "3:1"
   - halfTimeScore: string like "1:0"
   - NO predetermined score fields, NO simulated results
   - Response size: ~30KB

4. GET /8035/ranking?parentEventCategoryId=8035
   - Same as /8035/ranking, works with query param

## DEAD ENDPOINTS (all return 404):

ALL of the following return HTTP 404:
  /8035/schedule, /8035/live, /8035/fixtures, /8035/events, /8035/scores,
  /8035/rounds, /8035/rounds/current,
  /8035/round/{n}/playout (rounds 1-10, with and without parentEventCategoryId),
  /8035/round/{n}/schedule, /8035/round/{n}/results, /8035/round/{n}/live,
  /8035/round/{n}/matches, /8035/round/{n}/scores, /8035/round/{n}/events,
  /8035/playout, /8035/prematch, /8035/highlights, /8035/incidents,
  /8035/statistics, /8035/odds, /8035/markets, /8035/bettypes,
  /8035/standings, /8035/table,
  /8035/live/standings, /8035/live/table, /8035/live/matches,
  /8035/live/scores, /8035/live/events, /8035/live/playout
  (all tested with and without ?parentEventCategoryId=8035)

ALL alternate URL patterns return 404:
  parentEventCategoryId/8035/*, league/8035/*, eventCategory/8035/*
  /api/v1/instantleagues/*, /api/v2/instantleagues/*

ALL creative endpoints return 404:
  /8035/topscorers, /8035/players, /8035/teams, /8035/season,
  /8035/info, /8035/config, /8035/metadata, /8035/liveodds,
  /8035/lineup, /8035/lineups, /8035/commentary

## bet261.mg PROXY:
  Returns HTML frontend pages (status 200, text/html), NOT API data

## WEBSOCKET:
  All tested WebSocket URLs return 503 Service Unavailable:
    wss://hg-event-api-prod.sporty-tech.net/ws/instantleagues
    wss://hg-event-api-prod.sporty-tech.net/api/instantleagues/ws
    wss://hg-event-api-prod.sporty-tech.net/ws

## KEY FINDINGS:

1. The API has ONLY 3 working endpoints: /matches, /ranking, /results
2. There is NO "playout" endpoint at all (all return 404)
3. There are NO predetermined/predeterminedScore fields anywhere
4. There are NO simulated/predetermined results exposed via API
5. The canBeSimulated field exists on bet type items but is always false
6. /matches returns future rounds with betting data (no scores)
7. /results returns completed rounds with full goal-by-goal data
8. The "round" system uses expectedStart timestamps ~2 min apart
9. Rounds advance automatically (2-minute cycles based on expectedStart)
10. Current state: Round 10 is betting, Rounds 1-9 are completed

## DATA MODEL:

/matches response:
{
  rounds: [
    {
      id: number,
      eventCategoryId: 8035,
      expectedStart: ISO string (2-min intervals),
      expectedEnd: ISO string ("0001-01-01..." = no end),
      roundNumber: number,
      matches: [
        {
          id: number,
          entryPointId: 8035,
          round: string (e.g. "10"),
          name: "TeamA vs TeamB",
          awayTeam: { name, points, position, won, lost, draw },
          homeTeam: { name, points, position, won, lost, draw },
          expectedStart: ISO string,
          eventBetTypes: [ /* 14 bet types per match */ ]
        }
      ]
    }
  ],
  betTypes: [
    { id: 30083, name: "1X2" },
    { id: 30084, name: "Mi-tps 1X2" },
    /* ... 14 total bet types */
  ]
}

/results response:
{
  rounds: [
    {
      expectedStart: ISO string,
      expectedEnd: ISO string,
      roundNumber: number,
      matches: [
        {
          id: number,
          entryPointId: 8035,
          name: "TeamA vs TeamB",
          awayTeam: { name, points, position, won, lost, draw },
          homeTeam: { name, points, position, won, lost, draw },
          goals: [
            { minute: number, homeScore: number, awayScore: number, team: "Home"|"Away" }
          ],
          score: "3:1",
          halfTimeScore: "1:0",
          expectedStart: ISO string
        }
      ]
    }
  ],
  hasMore: boolean
}

/ranking response:
{
  teams: [
    { name, points, position, history: string[], won, lost, draw }
  ]
}
